#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2025-12-23-19-31-37}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/GdPEwbBgPz0/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=GdPEwbBgPz0](https://youtube.com/watch?v=GdPEwbBgPz0) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Tuesday, December 23, 2025, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Tuesday, December 23, 2025, 07:31 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Tuesday, December 23, 2025, 08:17 PM EST |
| Duration: | 46m 5s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 34 |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by George.  
>  
>Ninth prayer -- Dec. 23: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  though  you  are  rich  by  nature,  you  loved  the   
>poverty  of  our  humanity  when  you  became  man  like  us.   
>Though you were the descendant of kings and the heir of   
>David, the venerable, you were satisfied with a grotto and   
>a  humble  manger.    We  implore  you,  through  your  pure   
>nativity and through the intercession of your mother and   
>Saint  Joseph,  your  chosen  one,  to  give  us  a  love  of   
>voluntary  poverty,  that  we  may  be  satisfied  only  with   
>what  is  necessary  for  our  existence,  avoiding  waste  and   
>the love of an easy life, all the days of our lives. Amen.  
>  
>  
>  
>Ninth Oration -- Dec. 23: (classic)  
>  
>0 God, Who in Your very nature contain all the riches of heaven and earth,  
>You loved the poverty of humanity by choosing to become one of us,  
>You are the descendant of Kings and the Heir of David the Venerable.  
>You were satisfied to be born a stable and a humble manger.  
>   
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity   
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us an appreciation of voluntary poverty.  
>May we be satisfied with only that which is necessary for the maintenance of our lives.  
>Teach us to flee from excessive luxury and the love of abundance all the days of our lives.  
>  
>Amen  
>  
>  
>  
>Chapters:  
>[00:00:00](https://youtube.com/watch?v=GdPEwbBgPz0) Recording starts  
>[00:07:30](https://youtube.com/watch?v=GdPEwbBgPz0&t=7m30s) Glory to the Voice  
>[00:14:14](https://youtube.com/watch?v=GdPEwbBgPz0&t=14m14s) Oration  
>[00:17:36](https://youtube.com/watch?v=GdPEwbBgPz0&t=17m36s) Reflection by Bishop Gregory  
>[00:27:30](https://youtube.com/watch?v=GdPEwbBgPz0&t=27m30s) Arsal Allah

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2025-12-22-19-32-27}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/Hqbz1wUhrs4/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=Hqbz1wUhrs4](https://youtube.com/watch?v=Hqbz1wUhrs4) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Monday, December 22, 2025, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Monday, December 22, 2025, 07:32 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Monday, December 22, 2025, 08:27 PM EST |
| Duration: | 54m 56s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 26 |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Julian.  
>  
>Eighth prayer – Dec. 22: (modern)  
>  
>O King of Great Counsel, you have united the magnificence   
>of  your  resplendent  power  with  the  prudence  of  human   
>judgment when you, the almighty and powerful God, took   
>flight to Egypt to escape Herod. We implore you, through   
>your  pure  nativity  and  through  the  intercession  of  your   
>mother  and  Saint  Joseph,  your  chosen  one,  to  grant  us   
>sound judgment in all of our actions, that we may act in all   
>wisdom and serve you all the days of our lives. Amen.    
>  
>Eighth oration – Dec. 22: (classic)  
>  
>O King of Great Counsel,  
>You joined Your admirable power with the prudence of human judgment  
>when You, the Mighty and AII-Powerful God, fled into Egypt from the face of Herod.  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us good judgment in all our actions,  
>that we may think and act wisely all the days of our lives  
>as we subject ourselves to Your divine service.  
>  
>Amen  
>  
>

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Matthew.  
>  
>Sixth oration -- Dec. 20: (classic)  
>  
>O Word of God, Who comes  
>from the Mouth of God to be the  
>Life of all men; You Who became  
>Living Bread and was born in  
>Bethlehem, "The House of Bread",  
>to satisfy our hunger; We beseech  
>You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and the intercession of Your  
>Mother and Saint Joseph, Your  
>Chosen One, to grant us a piercing  
>hunger for that Bread which is  
>Your Pure Body and Blood.  May  
>we ever approach Your altar and  
>receive Your Sacred Mysteries  
>with fitting preparation so that our  
>Communion may be for us  
>salvation and life everlasting.  
>Amen.  
>  
>

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2025-12-19-19-25-27}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/WG7AIQIUAcE/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=WG7AIQIUAcE](https://youtube.com/watch?v=WG7AIQIUAcE) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Friday, December 19, 2025, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Friday, December 19, 2025, 07:25 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Friday, December 19, 2025, 08:25 PM EST |
| Duration: | 1h 15s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 37 |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Tony.  
>  
>Fourth prayer -- Dec. 19: (modern)  
>O Mighty One, seated in the heavenly heights,  
>you left the glory  of  your divinity and loved the lowliness   
>of our human nature to become our model of humility and   
>lowliness.  We  implore  you,  through  your  pure  nativity   
>and  through  the  intercession  of  your  Mother  and  Saint   
>Joseph, your chosen one, to give us humility of heart and   
>the  denial  of  ourselves,  that  we  may  remove  all  pride   
>which  prevents  us  from  knowing  you.  We  would  then   
>realize the weakness of our condition and glorify you, for   
>you  alone  are  holy  and  great  is  your  name.  To  you  be   
>glory, power, and strength. Amen.  
>  
>Fourth prayer -- Dec. 19: (classic)  
>  
>O Most High, by nature supreme and outranking all men and all things,  
>Who has left the magnificence of Your Divinity  
>and loved the lowliness of our humanity to become for us a  
>model in humility and lowliness;  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us humility of heart and an accurate estimation of ourselves.  
>Help us to conquer every show of false pride, which would have us choose  
>our own whims rather than Your Will.  
>May we realize that, compared to You, we are little indeed.  
>Glory be to You, for You alone are holy and great is Your Name.  
>To You be Glory, Magnificence and Power!  
>  
>Amen  
>  
>

>Cover art by Tony.  
>  
>Fourth prayer -- Dec. 18: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  Wine  of  the  Virgins  and  Lily  of  Purity,  through   
>your body all bodies are purified and all souls are clothed   
>in chastity. By dwelling in the womb of your mother, you   
>have  made  her  the  most  pure  and  glorious  of  all  virgins.   
>We implore you, through your pure nativity and through   
>the  intercession  of  your  mother  and  Saint  Joseph,  your   
>chosen one, to make our souls and bodies chaste and our   
>thoughts and actions pure, that we may serve you all the   
>days of our lives. Amen.    
>  
>  
>  
>Fourth oration -- Dec. 18: (classic)  
>  
>0 Wine of Virgins and Lily of Purity,  
>Who by a touch of Your hand heals the body and cleanses the soul;  
>Who by dwelling in the womb of Your Mother has made her the purest of the pure and most admirable among virgins;  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us to be pure in soul and body and clean in act clean and thought,  
>that we may serve You with a clean heart and pure body all the days of our lives.  
>  
>Amen  
>  
>  
>Chapters:

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2025-12-17-19-34-42}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/s0Dst11kv7E/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=s0Dst11kv7E](https://youtube.com/watch?v=s0Dst11kv7E) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Wednesday, December 17, 2025, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Wednesday, December 17, 2025, 07:34 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Wednesday, December 17, 2025, 08:26 PM EST |
| Duration: | 52m |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 38 |
| Reactions: | 1 like &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Joyce.  
>  
>Third prayer – Dec. 17 (modern)  
>  
>O Admirable Leader, you have guided the people to obey   
>you, not by harshness but by the tenderness of your love.   
>We implore you, through your pure nativity and through   
>the  intercession  of  your  Mother  and  Saint  Joseph,  your   
>chosen  one,  to  teach  us  perfect  obedience  to  your  holy   
>commandments  and  to  our  superiors,  not  for  fear  of   
>punishment,  or  constraint,  or  external  force,  but  by  the   
>assent  of  our  minds,  the  surrender  of  our  hearts,  the   
>enthusiasm of our spirits, and the joy of our souls. Amen.  
>  
>Third oration – Dec. 17 (classic)  
>  
>O Admirable Leader Who gains the obedience of Your people not by the severity of Your judgment but by the sweetness of Your love and Your welcome sojourn among us.  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to teach us complete obedience to Your holy commandments  
>and to submit to our superiors not for fear of punishment  
>but by a willing surrender of mind and heart, with gladness of heart and spirit.  
>  
>Amen  
>  
>

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Tony.  
>  
>Second oration -- Dec 16:  
>  
>O Hope of the Patriarchs and longing of the Gentiles,  
>in Your Nativity You have granted us hope.  
>The joy of this hope has called together the Shepherds,   
>the Magi and all believers in Your Holy Name,  
>and led them to adore You with all the acclaim of their hearts.   
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Virgin Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to keep us, by Your grace, from attachment to earthly goods.  
>Teach us not to depend only on ourselves and the weak ones of this world,  
>but to rely only on Your Fatherly direction and Your Divine Providence.   
>Attract our hearts and minds to reflect on Your heavenly riches   
>and to aspire and long for them above.  
>  
>Amen  
>

| Actual Start Time: | Monday, December 15, 2025, 07:30 PM EST |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>  
>First oration – Dec. 15:  
>  
>O Beautiful One in splendor,  
>fairer than the children of this world,  
>in Your inexplicable love for us, You became a man  
>to show us the invisible beauty of Your Divinity  
>in the splendor of Your visible body,  
>which You have taken from the Virgin Mary.  
>By this, You have attracted us to Your Divine Love,  
>You, Beloved of the Father and Only Delight of His Heart.  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Virgin Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to enkindle Your love in our hearts  
>and enlighten our minds with the rays of Your heavenly grace,  
>that we may love You with our whole heart and mind.  
>May we love everyone in You and for You,  
>You, Who alone are worthy of the love of all hearts,  
>  
>Amen  
>

>December 14, 2025: The Revelation to Joseph

>Page 748 -- Creed

#### The Finding of the Lord in the Temple[]{#d2025-01-05-11-26-11}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/hTi_jajnrZ4/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=hTi_jajnrZ4](https://youtube.com/watch?v=hTi_jajnrZ4) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Sunday, January  5, 2025, 11:30 AM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Sunday, January  5, 2025, 11:26 AM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Sunday, January  5, 2025, 12:58 PM EST |
| Duration: | 1h 32m 15s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 137 |
| Reactions: | 3 likes &#128077; |

>January 5, 2025: The Finding of the Lord in the Temple  
>Today's Divine Liturgy can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/sunday.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/sunday.pdf).  
>  
>Page 87 -- The Finding of Our Lord in the Temple  
>Page 748 -- Creed  
>Page 815 -- Anaphora of Saint John the Apostle  
>  
>-----  
>The Jubilee Prayer  
>  
>Father in heaven, may the faith you have given us  
>in your son, Jesus Christ, our brother,  
>and the flame of charity enkindled  
>in our hearts by the Holy Spirit,  
>reawaken in us the blessed hope   
>for the coming of your Kingdom.  
>  
>May your grace transform us  
>into tireless cultivators of the seeds of the Gospel.  
>May those seeds transform from within both humanity  
>and the whole cosmos in the sure expectation  
>of a new heaven and a new earth,  
>when, with the powers of Evil vanquished,  
>your glory will shine eternally.  
>  
>May the grace of the Jubilee  
>reawaken in us, Pilgrims of Hope,  
>a yearning for the treasures of heaven.  
>May that same grace spread the joy  
>and peace of our Redeemer throughout the earth.  
>To you our God, eternally blessed,   
>be glory and praise for ever.  Amen  
>  
>-----  
>  
>صلاة اليوبيل  
>  
>أيها الآب الذي في السماوات،  
>إن الإيمان الذي وهبتَه لنا  
>بابنك يسوع المسيح، أخانا،  
>وشعلة المحبة  
>المُنسكِبَة في قلوبنا بروحك القدوس،  
>يوقظان فينا الرجاء المبارك لمجيء ملكوتك.  
>   
>  
>إنّ نعمتك تحوّلنا إلى مزارعين مجتهدين للبذور الإنجيلية  
>التي تُخَمِّر الإنسانية والعالم،  
>في الانتظار الوثيق  
>للسماوات الجديدة والأرض الجديدة،  
>عندما تُهزَم قوى الشر  
>ويَظهَرُ مجدُك إلى الأبد.  
>  
> إنّ نعمة اليوبيل تحيي فينا  
>نحن، حجاج الرجاء،  
>الشوق نحو الخيرات السماوية  
>وتسكب على العالم أجمع  
>فرح وسلام فادينا.  
>أيها الإله الأزليّ المبارك،  
>لك الحمد والتسبيح إلى الأبد، آمين.  
>  
>Chapters:  
>[00:00:00](https://youtube.com/watch?v=hTi_jajnrZ4) Recording starts

>[00:09:01](https://youtube.com/watch?v=hTi_jajnrZ4&t=9m1s) Opening hymn

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2024-12-23-19-32-32}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/B7vdivHVLXM/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=B7vdivHVLXM](https://youtube.com/watch?v=B7vdivHVLXM) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Monday, December 23, 2024, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Monday, December 23, 2024, 07:32 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Monday, December 23, 2024, 08:20 PM EST |
| Duration: | 46m 15s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 53 |
| Reactions: | 3 likes &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by George.  
>  
>Ninth prayer -- Dec. 23: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  though  you  are  rich  by  nature,  you  loved  the   
>poverty  of  our  humanity  when  you  became  man  like  us.   
>Though you were the descendant of kings and the heir of   
>David, the venerable, you were satisfied with a grotto and   
>a  humble  manger.    We  implore  you,  through  your  pure   
>nativity and through the intercession of your mother and   
>Saint  Joseph,  your  chosen  one,  to  give  us  a  love  of   
>voluntary  poverty,  that  we  may  be  satisfied  only  with   
>what  is  necessary  for  our  existence,  avoiding  waste  and   
>the love of an easy life, all the days of our lives. Amen.    
>  
>  
>  
>Ninth Oration -- Dec. 23: (classic)  
>  
>0 God, Who in Your very nature contain all the riches of heaven and earth,  
>You loved the poverty of humanity by choosing to become one of us,  
>You are the descendant of Kings and the Heir of David the Venerable.  
>You were satisfied to be born a stable and a humble manger.  
>   
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity   
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us an appreciation of voluntary poverty.  
>May we be satisfied with only that which is necessary for the maintenance of our lives.  
>Teach us to flee from excessive luxury and the love of abundance all the days of our lives.  
>  
>Amen  
>  
>Chapters:

>[00:01:14](https://youtube.com/watch?v=B7vdivHVLXM&t=1m14s) Glory to the Word  
>[00:06:21](https://youtube.com/watch?v=B7vdivHVLXM&t=6m21s) Oration

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2024-12-21-19-28-26}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/JS6ycj-ua2Y/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=JS6ycj-ua2Y](https://youtube.com/watch?v=JS6ycj-ua2Y) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Saturday, December 21, 2024, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Saturday, December 21, 2024, 07:28 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Saturday, December 21, 2024, 08:22 PM EST |
| Duration: | 53m 55s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 39 |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Caroline.  
>  
>Seventh prayer -- Dec. 21: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  you  are  one  person  who  has  two  natures,  and   
>have taught us what you have heard from the Father. We   
>implore you, through your pure nativity and through the   
>intercession of your Mother and Saint Joseph, your chosen   
>one,  to  grant  us  a  living  faith  and  good  works  in   
>accordance  with  that  faith.  Do  not  permit  us  to  lose  the   
>reward of our faith because of our wrong doings, but may   
>we bear the fruits of true faith and good works. Amen.  
>  
>  
>Seventh oration -- Dec. 21: (classic)  
>  
>0 You Who are One person but also have the Nature of man  
>You Who have told us what You have heard from the Father;   
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity and the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One  
>to grant us an ardent belief in Your teachings and good acts to harmonize with them.  
>Do not permit us to lose the reward of our faith because of our own wrong doings.  
>Rather, make our lives fruitful in beliefs and good works.  
>  
>Amen  
>  
>Chapters:  
>[00:00:00](https://youtube.com/watch?v=JS6ycj-ua2Y) Recording starts

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2024-12-20-19-27-26}

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Matthew.  
>  
>Sixth oration -- Dec. 20: (classic)  
>  
>O Word of God, Who comes  
>from the Mouth of God to be the  
>Life of all men; You Who became  
>Living Bread and was born in  
>Bethlehem, "The House of Bread",  
>to satisfy our hunger; We beseech  
>You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and the intercession of Your  
>Mother and Saint Joseph, Your  
>Chosen One, to grant us a piercing  
>hunger for that Bread which is  
>Your Pure Body and Blood.  May  
>we ever approach Your altar and  
>receive Your Sacred Mysteries  
>with fitting preparation so that our  
>Communion may be for us  
>salvation and life everlasting.  
>Amen.  
>  
>Chapters:

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2024-12-19-19-32-32}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/YrbeNil0jK8/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=YrbeNil0jK8](https://youtube.com/watch?v=YrbeNil0jK8) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Thursday, December 19, 2024, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Thursday, December 19, 2024, 07:32 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Thursday, December 19, 2024, 08:19 PM EST |
| Duration: | 24m 35s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 45 |
| Reactions: | 1 like &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Tony.  
>  
>Fourth prayer -- Dec. 19: (modern)  
>O Mighty One, seated in the heavenly heights,  
>you left the glory  of  your divinity and loved the lowliness   
>of our human nature to become our model of humility and   
>lowliness.  We  implore  you,  through  your  pure  nativity   
>and  through  the  intercession  of  your  Mother  and  Saint   
>Joseph, your chosen one, to give us humility of heart and   
>the  denial  of  ourselves,  that  we  may  remove  all  pride   
>which  prevents  us  from  knowing  you.  We  would  then   
>realize the weakness of our condition and glorify you, for   
>you  alone  are  holy  and  great  is  your  name.  To  you  be   
>glory, power, and strength. Amen.  
>  
>Fourth prayer -- Dec. 19: (classic)  
>  
>O Most High, by nature supreme and outranking all men and all things,  
>Who has left the magnificence of Your Divinity  
>and loved the lowliness of our humanity to become for us a  
>model in humility and lowliness;  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us humility of heart and an accurate estimation of ourselves.  
>Help us to conquer every show of false pride, which would have us choose  
>our own whims rather than Your Will.  
>May we realize that, compared to You, we are little indeed.  
>Glory be to You, for You alone are holy and great is Your Name.  
>To You be Glory, Magnificence and Power!  
>  
>Amen  
>  
>Chapters:

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2024-12-18-19-23-27}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/4Ekhpb9qDww/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=4Ekhpb9qDww](https://youtube.com/watch?v=4Ekhpb9qDww) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Wednesday, December 18, 2024, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Wednesday, December 18, 2024, 07:23 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Wednesday, December 18, 2024, 08:17 PM EST |
| Duration: | 54m |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 51 |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Tony.  
>  
>Fourth prayer -- Dec. 18: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  Wine  of  the  Virgins  and  Lily  of  Purity,  through   
>your body all bodies are purified and all souls are clothed   
>in chastity. By dwelling in the womb of your mother, you   
>have  made  her  the  most  pure  and  glorious  of  all  virgins.   
>We implore you, through your pure nativity and through   
>the  intercession  of  your  mother  and  Saint  Joseph,  your   
>chosen one, to make our souls and bodies chaste and our   
>thoughts and actions pure, that we may serve you all the   
>days of our lives. Amen.    
>  
>  
>  
>Fourth oration -- Dec. 18: (classic)  
>  
>0 Wine of Virgins and Lily of Purity,  
>Who by a touch of Your hand heals the body and cleanses the soul;  
>Who by dwelling in the womb of Your Mother has made her the purest of the pure and most admirable among virgins;  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us to be pure in soul and body and clean in act clean and thought,  
>that we may serve You with a clean heart and pure body all the days of our lives.  
>  
>Amen  
>  
>

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2024-12-17-19-26-12}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/VrEiwEra-sg/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=VrEiwEra-sg](https://youtube.com/watch?v=VrEiwEra-sg) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Tuesday, December 17, 2024, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Tuesday, December 17, 2024, 07:26 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Tuesday, December 17, 2024, 08:11 PM EST |
| Duration: | 45m 21s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 47 |
| Reactions: | 1 like &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Joyce.  
>  
>Third prayer – Dec. 17 (modern)  
>  
>O Admirable Leader, you have guided the people to obey   
>you, not by harshness but by the tenderness of your love.   
>We implore you, through your pure nativity and through   
>the  intercession  of  your  Mother  and  Saint  Joseph,  your   
>chosen  one,  to  teach  us  perfect  obedience  to  your  holy   
>commandments  and  to  our  superiors,  not  for  fear  of   
>punishment,  or  constraint,  or  external  force,  but  by  the   
>assent  of  our  minds,  the  surrender  of  our  hearts,  the   
>enthusiasm of our spirits, and the joy of our souls. Amen.  
>  
>Third oration – Dec. 17 (classic)  
>  
>O Admirable Leader Who gains the obedience of Your people not by the severity of Your judgment but by the sweetness of Your love and Your welcome sojourn among us.  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to teach us complete obedience to Your holy commandments  
>and to submit to our superiors not for fear of punishment  
>but by a willing surrender of mind and heart, with gladness of heart and spirit.  
>  
>Amen  
>

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2024-12-16-19-33-27}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/oFLTvEna0Yw/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=oFLTvEna0Yw](https://youtube.com/watch?v=oFLTvEna0Yw) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Monday, December 16, 2024, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Monday, December 16, 2024, 07:33 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Monday, December 16, 2024, 08:16 PM EST |
| Duration: | 42m 50s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 53 |
| Reactions: | 1 like &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Tony.  
>  
>Second oration -- Dec 16:  
>  
>O Hope of the Patriarchs and longing of the Gentiles,  
>in Your Nativity You have granted us hope.  
>The joy of this hope has called together the Shepherds,   
>the Magi and all believers in Your Holy Name,  
>and led them to adore You with all the acclaim of their hearts.   
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Virgin Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to keep us, by Your grace, from attachment to earthly goods.  
>Teach us not to depend only on ourselves and the weak ones of this world,  
>but to rely only on Your Fatherly direction and Your Divine Providence.   
>Attract our hearts and minds to reflect on Your heavenly riches   
>and to aspire and long for them above.  
>  
>Amen  
>

>Dear Parishioners & Friends,  
>  
>We received the sad news of the passing of Fortunée Lutfi to eternal life. She is the widow of Bahjat Lutfi, the mother of Edouard, Najib, Rita, Leila, Haifa, Shouly and Jacqueline (deceased). Her sisters are Linda Shouela and Susan Hadriye and her deceased brothers are Joseph, Isaac, Albert and Gaby Mizrahi.  
>  
>Visiting hours will be held at Our Lady of Lebanon Cathedral (113 Remsen Street, Brooklyn, NY 11201) on Sunday, May 5th, from  3 to 7 pm followed by the Prayer of Incense and a mercy meal.  
>  
>The Funeral Divine Liturgy will be held the next day at 10 am at Our Lady of Lebanon Cathedral (113 Remsen St., Brooklyn, NY 11201) followed by the burial at Forest Green Park Cemetery (535 Texas Rd., Morganville, NJ 07551)  
>  
>  
>May a choir of angels accompany Fortunée to the heavenly abode with the righteous and just.  
>  
>Abouna Dominique Hanna  
>Our Lady of Lebanon Maronite Cathedral   
>  
>Brooklyn, NY  
>  
>  
>“By his death, Christ preserved us from corruption, and by his resurrection he renewed our image and granted us eternal life in his kingdom.” ­The Book of Offering

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2023-12-23-19-25-56}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/eV9EJsu4wZ8/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=eV9EJsu4wZ8](https://youtube.com/watch?v=eV9EJsu4wZ8) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Saturday, December 23, 2023, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Saturday, December 23, 2023, 07:25 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Saturday, December 23, 2023, 08:23 PM EST |
| Duration: | 57m 30s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 37 |
| Reactions: | 1 like &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by George.  
>  
>Ninth prayer -- Dec. 23: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  though  you  are  rich  by  nature,  you  loved  the   
>poverty  of  our  humanity  when  you  became  man  like  us.   
>Though you were the descendant of kings and the heir of   
>David, the venerable, you were satisfied with a grotto and   
>a  humble  manger.    We  implore  you,  through  your  pure   
>nativity and through the intercession of your mother and   
>Saint  Joseph,  your  chosen  one,  to  give  us  a  love  of   
>voluntary  poverty,  that  we  may  be  satisfied  only  with   
>what  is  necessary  for  our  existence,  avoiding  waste  and   
>the love of an easy life, all the days of our lives. Amen.    
>  
>  
>  
>Ninth Oration -- Dec. 23: (classic)  
>  
>0 God, Who in Your very nature contain all the riches of heaven and earth,  
>You loved the poverty of humanity by choosing to become one of us,  
>You are the descendant of Kings and the Heir of David the Venerable.  
>You were satisfied to be born a stable and a humble manger.  
>   
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity   
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us an appreciation of voluntary poverty.  
>May we be satisfied with only that which is necessary for the maintenance of our lives.  
>Teach us to flee from excessive luxury and the love of abundance all the days of our lives.  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2023-12-22-19-24-22}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/DNBiQdstzHs/mqdefault.jpg)

| Scheduled Start Time: | Friday, December 22, 2023, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Friday, December 22, 2023, 07:24 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Friday, December 22, 2023, 08:22 PM EST |
| Duration: | 58m |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Tony.  
>  
>Eighth prayer -- Dec. 22: (modern)  
>  
>O King of Great Counsel, you have united the magnificence   
>of  your  resplendent  power  with  the  prudence  of  human   
>judgment when you, the almighty and powerful God, took   
>flight to Egypt to escape Herod. We implore you, through   
>your  pure  nativity  and  through  the  intercession  of  your   
>mother  and  Saint  Joseph,  your  chosen  one,  to  grant  us   
>sound judgment in all of our actions, that we may act in all   
>wisdom and serve you all the days of our lives. Amen.    
>  
>  
>  
>Eighth oration -- Dec. 22: (classic)  
>  
>O King of Great Counsel,  
>You joined Your admirable power with the prudence of human judgment  
>when You, the Mighty and AII-Powerful God, fled into Egypt from the face of Herod.  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,   
>to grant us good judgment in all our actions,  
>that we may think and act wisely all the days of our lives  
>as we subject ourselves to Your divine service.  
>  
>Amen

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/kmwLVhX8PeE/mqdefault.jpg)

| Scheduled Start Time: | Thursday, December 21, 2023, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Thursday, December 21, 2023, 07:32 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Thursday, December 21, 2023, 08:22 PM EST |
| Duration: | 50m 21s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 38 |
| Reactions: | 1 like &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Caroline.  
>  
>Seventh prayer -- Dec. 21: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  you  are  one  person  who  has  two  natures,  and   
>have taught us what you have heard from the Father. We   
>implore you, through your pure nativity and through the   
>intercession of your Mother and Saint Joseph, your chosen   
>one,  to  grant  us  a  living  faith  and  good  works  in   
>accordance  with  that  faith.  Do  not  permit  us  to  lose  the   
>reward of our faith because of our wrong doings, but may   
>we bear the fruits of true faith and good works. Amen.  
>  
>  
>Seventh oration -- Dec. 21: (classic)  
>  
>0 You Who are One person but also have the Nature of man  
>You Who have told us what You have heard from the Father;   
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity and the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One  
>to grant us an ardent belief in Your teachings and good acts to harmonize with them.  
>Do not permit us to lose the reward of our faith because of our own wrong doings.  
>Rather, make our lives fruitful in beliefs and good works.  
>  
>Amen

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Matthew.  
>  
>Sixth oration -- Dec. 20: (classic)  
>  
>O Word of God, Who comes  
>from the Mouth of God to be the  
>Life of all men; You Who became  
>Living Bread and was born in  
>Bethlehem, "The House of Bread",  
>to satisfy our hunger; We beseech  
>You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and the intercession of Your  
>Mother and Saint Joseph, Your  
>Chosen One, to grant us a piercing  
>hunger for that Bread which is  
>Your Pure Body and Blood.  May  
>we ever approach Your altar and  
>receive Your Sacred Mysteries  
>with fitting preparation so that our  
>Communion may be for us  
>salvation and life everlasting.  
>Amen.

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2023-12-19-19-31-24}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/Rup21YQga9k/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=Rup21YQga9k](https://youtube.com/watch?v=Rup21YQga9k) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Tuesday, December 19, 2023, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Tuesday, December 19, 2023, 07:31 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Tuesday, December 19, 2023, 08:25 PM EST |
| Duration: | 54m 2s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 49 |
| Reactions: | 2 likes &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Christelle.  
>  
>Fourth prayer -- Dec. 19: (modern)  
>O Mighty One, seated in the heavenly heights,  
>you left the glory  of  your divinity and loved the lowliness   
>of our human nature to become our model of humility and   
>lowliness.  We  implore  you,  through  your  pure  nativity   
>and  through  the  intercession  of  your  Mother  and  Saint   
>Joseph, your chosen one, to give us humility of heart and   
>the  denial  of  ourselves,  that  we  may  remove  all  pride   
>which  prevents  us  from  knowing  you.  We  would  then   
>realize the weakness of our condition and glorify you, for   
>you  alone  are  holy  and  great  is  your  name.  To  you  be   
>glory, power, and strength. Amen.  
>  
>Fourth prayer -- Dec. 19: (classic)  
>  
>O Most High, by nature supreme and outranking all men and all things,  
>Who has left the magnificence of Your Divinity  
>and loved the lowliness of our humanity to become for us a  
>model in humility and lowliness;  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us humility of heart and an accurate estimation of ourselves.  
>Help us to conquer every show of false pride, which would have us choose  
>our own whims rather than Your Will.  
>May we realize that, compared to You, we are little indeed.  
>Glory be to You, for You alone are holy and great is Your Name.  
>To You be Glory, Magnificence and Power!  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2023-12-18-19-24-58}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/zK68z9lyKBw/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=zK68z9lyKBw](https://youtube.com/watch?v=zK68z9lyKBw) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Monday, December 18, 2023, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Monday, December 18, 2023, 07:24 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Monday, December 18, 2023, 08:21 PM EST |
| Duration: | 56m 41s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 32 |
| Reactions: | 1 like &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Tony.  
>  
>Fourth prayer -- Dec. 18: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  Wine  of  the  Virgins  and  Lily  of  Purity,  through   
>your body all bodies are purified and all souls are clothed   
>in chastity. By dwelling in the womb of your mother, you   
>have  made  her  the  most  pure  and  glorious  of  all  virgins.   
>We implore you, through your pure nativity and through   
>the  intercession  of  your  mother  and  Saint  Joseph,  your   
>chosen one, to make our souls and bodies chaste and our   
>thoughts and actions pure, that we may serve you all the   
>days of our lives. Amen.    
>  
>  
>  
>Fourth oration -- Dec. 18: (classic)  
>  
>0 Wine of Virgins and Lily of Purity,  
>Who by a touch of Your hand heals the body and cleanses the soul;  
>Who by dwelling in the womb of Your Mother has made her the purest of the pure and most admirable among virgins;  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us to be pure in soul and body and clean in act clean and thought,  
>that we may serve You with a clean heart and pure body all the days of our lives.  
>  
>Amen

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/5LWhrB4YSkw/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=5LWhrB4YSkw](https://youtube.com/watch?v=5LWhrB4YSkw) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Saturday, December 16, 2023, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Saturday, December 16, 2023, 07:26 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Saturday, December 16, 2023, 08:23 PM EST |
| Duration: | 57m 26s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 59 |
| Reactions: | 1 like &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Samantha.  
>  
>Second oration -- Dec 16:  
>  
>O Hope of the Patriarchs and longing of the Gentiles,  
>in Your Nativity You have granted us hope.  
>The joy of this hope has called together the Shepherds,   
>the Magi and all believers in Your Holy Name,  
>and led them to adore You with all the acclaim of their hearts.   
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Virgin Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to keep us, by Your grace, from attachment to earthly goods.  
>Teach us not to depend only on ourselves and the weak ones of this world,  
>but to rely only on Your Fatherly direction and Your Divine Providence.   
>Attract our hearts and minds to reflect on Your heavenly riches   
>and to aspire and long for them above.  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2023-12-15-20-07-59}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/jYAt8iowGdo/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=jYAt8iowGdo](https://youtube.com/watch?v=jYAt8iowGdo) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Friday, December 15, 2023, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Friday, December 15, 2023, 08:07 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Friday, December 15, 2023, 08:23 PM EST |
| Duration: | 15m 15s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 23 |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>  
>First oration -- Dec. 15:  
>  
>O Beautiful One in splendor,  
>fairer than the children of this world,   
>in Your inexplicable love for us, You became a man  
>to show us the invisible beauty of Your Divinity  
>in the splendor of Your visible body,  
>which You have taken from the Virgin Mary.  
>By this, You have attracted us to Your Divine Love,  
>You, Beloved of the Father and Only Delight of His Heart.   
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Virgin Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to enkindle Your love in our hearts  
>and enlighten our minds with the rays of Your heavenly grace,  
>that we may love You with our whole heart and mind.  
>May we love everyone in You and for You,   
>You, Who alone are worthy of the love of all hearts,  
>  
>Amen

#### Sunday of the Birth of John the Baptist[]{#d2023-12-10-11-17-58}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/867kSikCN6M/mqdefault.jpg)

|||

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2022-12-23-19-19-53}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/DM6H3phiAHY/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=DM6H3phiAHY](https://youtube.com/watch?v=DM6H3phiAHY) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Friday, December 23, 2022, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Friday, December 23, 2022, 07:19 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Friday, December 23, 2022, 08:19 PM EST |
| Duration: | 59m 51s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 58 |
| Reactions: | 1 like &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by William  
>  
>Ninth prayer -- Dec. 23: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  though  you  are  rich  by  nature,  you  loved  the   
>poverty  of  our  humanity  when  you  became  man  like  us.   
>Though you were the descendent of kings and the heir of   
>David, the venerable, you were satisfied with a grotto and   
>a  humble  manger.    We  implore  you,  through  your  pure   
>nativity and through the intercession of your mother and   
>Saint  Joseph,  your  chosen  one,  to  give  us  a  love  of   
>voluntary  poverty,  that  we  may  be  satisfied  only  with   
>what  is  necessary  for  our  existence,  avoiding  waste  and   
>the love of an easy life, all the days of our lives. Amen.    
>  
>  
>  
>Ninth Oration -- Dec. 23: (classic)  
>  
>0 God, Who in Your very nature contain all the riches of heaven and earth,  
>You loved the poverty of humanity by choosing to become one of us,  
>You are the descendant of Kings and the Heir of David the Venerable.  
>You were satisfied to be born a stable and a humble manger.  
>   
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity   
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us an appreciation of voluntary poverty.  
>May we be satisfied with only that which is necessary for the maintenance of our lives.  
>Teach us to flee from excessive luxury and the love of abundance all the days of our lives.  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2022-12-22-19-26-17}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/HOuaTlmyJAw/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=HOuaTlmyJAw](https://youtube.com/watch?v=HOuaTlmyJAw) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Thursday, December 22, 2022, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Thursday, December 22, 2022, 07:26 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Thursday, December 22, 2022, 08:24 PM EST |
| Duration: | 58m 51s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 43 |
| Reactions: | 1 like &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Julian.  
>  
>Eighth prayer -- Dec. 22: (modern)  
>  
>O King of Great Counsel, you have united the magnificence   
>of  your  resplendent  power  with  the  prudence  of  human   
>judgment when you, the almighty and powerful God, took   
>flight to Egypt to escape Herod. We implore you, through   
>your  pure  nativity  and  through  the  intercession  of  your   
>mother  and  Saint  Joseph,  your  chosen  one,  to  grant  us   
>sound judgment in all of our actions, that we may act in all   
>wisdom and serve you all the days of our lives. Amen.    
>  
>  
>  
>Eighth oration -- Dec. 22: (classic)  
>  
>O King of Great Counsel,  
>You joined Your admirable power with the prudence of humuan judgment  
>when You, the Mighty and AII-Powerful God, fled into Egypt from the face of Herod.  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,   
>to grant us good judgment in all our actions,  
>that we may think and act wisely all the days of our lives  
>as we subject ourselves to Your divine service.  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2022-12-21-19-34-38}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/5sZTRyEsNkU/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=5sZTRyEsNkU](https://youtube.com/watch?v=5sZTRyEsNkU) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Wednesday, December 21, 2022, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Wednesday, December 21, 2022, 07:34 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Wednesday, December 21, 2022, 08:23 PM EST |
| Duration: | 48m 36s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 30 |
| Reactions: | 1 like &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Nisreen.  
>  
>Seventh prayer -- Dec. 21: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  you  are  one  person  who  has  two  natures,  and   
>have taught us what you have heard from the Father. We   
>implore you, through your pure nativity and through the   
>intercession of your Mother and Saint Joseph, your chosen   
>one,  to  grant  us  a  living  faith  and  good  works  in   
>accordance  with  that  faith.  Do  not  permit  us  to  lose  the   
>reward of our faith because of our wrong doings, but may   
>we bear the fruits of true faith and good works. Amen.  
>  
>  
>Seventh oration -- Dec. 21: (classic)  
>  
>0 You Who are One person but also have the Nature of man  
>You Who have told us what You have heard from the Father;   
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity and the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One  
>to grant us an ardent belief in Your teachings and good acts to harmonize with them.  
>Do not permit us to lose the reward of our faith because of our own wrong doings.  
>Rather, make our lives fruitful in beliefs and good works.  
>  
>Amen

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/PCFpJugz9f0/mqdefault.jpg)

| Scheduled Start Time: | Tuesday, December 20, 2022, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Tuesday, December 20, 2022, 07:24 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Tuesday, December 20, 2022, 08:23 PM EST |
| Duration: | 59m 45s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 48 |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Matthew.  
>  
>Sixth oration -- Dec. 20: (classic)  
>  
>O Word of God, Who comes  
>from the Mouth of God to be the  
>Life of all men; You Who became  
>Living Bread and was born in  
>Bethlehem, "The House of Bread",  
>to satisfy our hunger; We beseech  
>You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and the intercession of Your  
>Mother and Saint Joseph, Your  
>Chosen One, to grant us a piercing  
>hunger for that Bread which is  
>Your Pure Body and Blood.  May  
>we ever approach Your altar and  
>receive Your Sacred Mysteries  
>with fitting preparation so that our  
>Communion may be for us  
>salvation and life everlasting.  
>Amen.

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2022-12-19-19-28-47}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/6lCU14WIYiU/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=6lCU14WIYiU](https://youtube.com/watch?v=6lCU14WIYiU) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Monday, December 19, 2022, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Monday, December 19, 2022, 07:28 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Monday, December 19, 2022, 08:17 PM EST |
| Duration: | 48m 54s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 34 |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Christelle.  
>  
>Fourth prayer -- Dec. 19: (modern)  
>O Mighty One, seated in the heavenly heights,  
>you left the glory  of  your divinity and loved the lowliness   
>of our human nature to become our model of humility and   
>lowliness.  We  implore  you,  through  your  pure  nativity   
>and  through  the  intercession  of  your  Mother  and  Saint   
>Joseph, your chosen one, to give us humility of heart and   
>the  denial  of  ourselves,  that  we  may  remove  all  pride   
>which  prevents  us  from  knowing  you.  We  would  then   
>realize the weakness of our condition and glorify you, for   
>you  alone  are  holy  and  great  is  your  name.  To  you  be   
>glory, power, and strength. Amen.  
>  
>Fourth prayer -- Dec. 19: (classic)  
>  
>O Most High, by nature supreme and outranking all men and all things,  
>Who has left the magnificence of Your Divinity  
>and loved the lowliness of our humanity to become for us a  
>model in humility and lowliness;  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us humility of heart and an accurate estimation of ourselves.  
>Help us to conquer every show of false pride, which would have us choose  
>our own whims rather than Your Will.  
>May we realize that, compared to You, we are little indeed.  
>Glory be to You, for You alone are holy and great is Your Name.  
>To You be Glory, Magnificence and Power!  
>  
>Amen

#### The Genealogy of Jesus[]{#d2022-12-18-11-18-28}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/YHCUAleA9W4/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2022-12-17-19-31-22}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/27sh9mCpwbs/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=27sh9mCpwbs](https://youtube.com/watch?v=27sh9mCpwbs) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Saturday, December 17, 2022, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Saturday, December 17, 2022, 07:31 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Saturday, December 17, 2022, 08:20 PM EST |
| Duration: | 49m 16s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 46 |
| Reactions: | 3 likes &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Joyce.  
>  
>  
>  
>Third prayer -- Dec. 17 (modern)  
>  
>O Admirable Leader, you have guided the people to obey   
>you, not by harshness but by the tenderness of your love.   
>We implore you, through your pure nativity and through   
>the  intercession  of  your  Mother  and  Saint  Joseph,  your   
>chosen  one,  to  teach  us  perfect  obedience  to  your  holy   
>commandments  and  to  our  superiors,  not  for  fear  of   
>punishment,  or  constraint,  or  external  force,  but  by  the   
>assent  of  our  minds,  the  surrender  of  our  hearts,  the   
>enthusiasm of our spirits, and the joy of our souls. Amen.  
>  
>  
>  
>  
>  
>  
>Third oration -- Dec. 17 (classic)  
>  
>O Admirable Leader Who gains the obedience of Your people not by the severity of Your judgment  
>but by the sweetness of Your love and Your welcome sojourn among us.  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to teach us complete obedience to Your holy commandments  
>and to submit to our superiors not for fear of punishment  
>but by a willing surrender of mind and heart, with gladness of heart and spirit.  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2022-12-16-19-28-07}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/h3T4dDXrg8Q/mqdefault.jpg)

|||

| Scheduled Start Time: | Friday, December 16, 2022, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Friday, December 16, 2022, 07:28 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Friday, December 16, 2022, 08:23 PM EST |
| Duration: | 55m 26s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Marielle.  
>  
>Second oration -- Dec 16:  
>  
>O Hope of the Patriarchs and longing of the Gentiles,  
>in Your Nativity You have granted us hope.  
>The joy of this hope has called together the Shepherds,   
>the Magi and all believers in Your Holy Name,  
>and led them to adore You with all the acclaim of their hearts.   
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Virgin Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to keep us, by Your grace, from attachment to earthly goods.  
>Teach us not to depend only on ourselves and the weak ones of this world,  
>but to rely only on Your Fatherly direction and Your Divine Providence.   
>Attract our hearts and minds to reflect on Your heavenly riches   
>and to aspire and long for them above.  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2022-12-15-19-27-33}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/Hl6p8SBYdgY/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=Hl6p8SBYdgY](https://youtube.com/watch?v=Hl6p8SBYdgY) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Thursday, December 15, 2022, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Thursday, December 15, 2022, 07:27 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Thursday, December 15, 2022, 08:23 PM EST |
| Duration: | 56m 6s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 54 |
| Reactions: | 1 like &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>  
>First oration -- Dec. 15:  
>  
>O Beautiful One in splendor,  
>fairer than the children of this world,   
>in Your inexplicable love for us, You became a man  
>to show us the invisible beauty of Your Divinity  
>in the splendor of Your visible body,  
>which You have taken from the Virgin Mary.  
>By this, You have attracted us to Your Divine Love,  
>You, Beloved of the Father and Only Delight of His Heart.   
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Virgin Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to enkindle Your love in our hearts  
>and enlighten our minds with the rays of Your heavenly grace,  
>that we may love You with our whole heart and mind.  
>May we love everyone in You and for You,   
>You, Who alone are worthy of the love of all hearts,  
>  
>Amen

>Dear Parishioners & Friends,  
>  
>It is with great sorrow that we announce the passing of John Tremmel.   
>  
>The viewing will be held at Cobble Hill Funeral Home ( 171 Court St . , Brooklyn, NY 11201 ) on Thursday, January 20th from 10:30 to 11:30 am.    
>  
>  
>The Funeral will be held at Our Lady of Lebanon Cathedral the same day (Thursday, January 20th) at 12 noon followed by the burial at Calvary Cemeteries (49-02 Laurel Hill Blvd., Woodside, NY 11377).    
>  
>May a choir of angels accompany John to the heavenly abode with the righteous and just.   
>  
>Abouna Dominique Hanna  
>Our Lady of Lebanon Maronite Cathedral   
>  
>Brooklyn, NY  
>  
>   
>“By Your voice, you awakened the righteous and the just who had gone to their rest in the sleep of death.” ­The Book of Offering

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2021-12-23-19-24-47}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/DqKZjv2b6tM/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=DqKZjv2b6tM](https://youtube.com/watch?v=DqKZjv2b6tM) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Thursday, December 23, 2021, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Thursday, December 23, 2021, 07:24 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Thursday, December 23, 2021, 10:08 PM EST |
| Duration: | 44m |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 51 |
| Reactions: | 2 dislikes &#128078; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by William  
>  
>Ninth prayer -- Dec. 23: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  though  you  are  rich  by  nature,  you  loved  the   
>poverty  of  our  humanity  when  you  became  man  like  us.   
>Though you were the descendent of kings and the heir of   
>David, the venerable, you were satisfied with a grotto and   
>a  humble  manger.    We  implore  you,  through  your  pure   
>nativity and through the intercession of your mother and   
>Saint  Joseph,  your  chosen  one,  to  give  us  a  love  of   
>voluntary  poverty,  that  we  may  be  satisfied  only  with   
>what  is  necessary  for  our  existence,  avoiding  waste  and   
>the love of an easy life, all the days of our lives. Amen.    
>  
>  
>  
>Ninth Oration -- Dec. 23: (classic)  
>  
>0 God, Who in Your very nature contain all the riches of heaven and earth,  
>You loved the poverty of humanity by choosing to become one of us,  
>You are the descendant of Kings and the Heir of David the Venerable.  
>You were satisfied to be born a stable and a humble manger.  
>   
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity   
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us an appreciation of voluntary poverty.  
>May we be satisfied with only that which is necessary for the maintenance of our lives.  
>Teach us to flee from excessive luxury and the love of abundance all the days of our lives.  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2021-12-22-19-33-48}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/MqRzOwk7VXQ/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=MqRzOwk7VXQ](https://youtube.com/watch?v=MqRzOwk7VXQ) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Wednesday, December 22, 2021, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Wednesday, December 22, 2021, 07:33 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Wednesday, December 22, 2021, 10:34 PM EST |
| Duration: | 1h 40m 56s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 19 |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Julian.  
>  
>Eighth prayer -- Dec. 22: (modern)  
>  
>O King of Great Counsel, you have united the magnificence   
>of  your  resplendent  power  with  the  prudence  of  human   
>judgment when you, the almighty and powerful God, took   
>flight to Egypt to escape Herod. We implore you, through   
>your  pure  nativity  and  through  the  intercession  of  your   
>mother  and  Saint  Joseph,  your  chosen  one,  to  grant  us   
>sound judgment in all of our actions, that we may act in all   
>wisdom and serve you all the days of our lives. Amen.    
>  
>  
>  
>Eighth oration -- Dec. 22: (classic)  
>  
>O King of Great Counsel,  
>You joined Your admirable power with the prudence of humuan judgment  
>when You, the Mighty and AII-Powerful God, fled into Egypt from the face of Herod.  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,   
>to grant us good judgment in all our actions,  
>that we may think and act wisely all the days of our lives  
>as we subject ourselves to Your divine service.  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2021-12-21-19-35-30}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/_whauZ-fdQg/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=_whauZ-fdQg](https://youtube.com/watch?v=_whauZ-fdQg) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Tuesday, December 21, 2021, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Tuesday, December 21, 2021, 07:35 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Tuesday, December 21, 2021, 10:03 PM EST |
| Duration: | 48m 1s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 13 |
| Reactions: | 1 dislike &#128078; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Nisreen.  
>  
>Seventh prayer -- Dec. 21: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  you  are  one  person  who  has  two  natures,  and   
>have taught us what you have heard from the Father. We   
>implore you, through your pure nativity and through the   
>intercession of your Mother and Saint Joseph, your chosen   
>one,  to  grant  us  a  living  faith  and  good  works  in   
>accordance  with  that  faith.  Do  not  permit  us  to  lose  the   
>reward of our faith because of our wrong doings, but may   
>we bear the fruits of true faith and good works. Amen.  
>  
>  
>Seventh oration -- Dec. 21: (classic)  
>  
>0 You Who are One person but also have the Nature of man  
>You Who have told us what You have heard from the Father;   
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity and the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One  
>to grant us an ardent belief in Your teachings and good acts to harmonize with them.  
>Do not permit us to lose the reward of our faith because of our own wrong doings.  
>Rather, make our lives fruitful in beliefs and good works.  
>  
>Amen

| Actual Start Time: | Monday, December 20, 2021, 07:24 PM EST |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Matthew.  
>  
>Sixth prayer -- Dec. 20: (modern)  
>  
>  
>Sixth oration -- Dec. 20: (classic)  
>  
>O Word of God, Who comes  
>from the Mouth of God to be the  
>Life of all men; You Who became  
>Living Bread and was born in  
>Bethlehem, "The House of Bread",  
>to satisfy our hunger; We beseech  
>You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and the intercession of Your  
>Mother and Saint Joseph, Your  
>Chosen One, to grant us a piercing  
>hunger for that Bread which is  
>Your Pure Body and Blood.  May  
>we ever approach Your altar and  
>receive Your Sacred Mysteries  
>with fitting preparation so that our  
>Communion may be for us  
>salvation and life everlasting.  
>Amen.

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2021-12-18-19-26-32}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/AUs2VRstq2M/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=AUs2VRstq2M](https://youtube.com/watch?v=AUs2VRstq2M) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Saturday, December 18, 2021, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Saturday, December 18, 2021, 07:26 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Saturday, December 18, 2021, 08:08 PM EST |
| Duration: | 41m 35s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 32 |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Christelle.  
>  
>Fourth prayer -- Dec. 18: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  Wine  of  the  Virgins  and  Lily  of  Purity,  through   
>your body all bodies are purified and all souls are clothed   
>in chastity. By dwelling in the womb of your mother, you   
>have  made  her  the  most  pure  and  glorious  of  all  virgins.   
>We implore you, through your pure nativity and through   
>the  intercession  of  your  mother  and  Saint  Joseph,  your   
>chosen one, to make our souls and bodies chaste and our   
>thoughts and actions pure, that we may serve you all the   
>days of our lives. Amen.    
>  
>  
>  
>Fourth oration -- Dec. 18: (classic)  
>  
>0 Wine of Virgins and Lily of Purity,  
>Who by a touch of Your hand heals the body and cleanses the soul;  
>Who by dwelling in the womb of Your Mother has made her the purest of the pure and most admirable among virgins;  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us to be pure in soul and body and clean in act clean and thought,  
>that we may serve You with a clean heart and pure body all the days of our lives.  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2021-12-17-19-28-53}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/mDu9i23mCaE/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=mDu9i23mCaE](https://youtube.com/watch?v=mDu9i23mCaE) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Friday, December 17, 2021, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Friday, December 17, 2021, 07:28 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Friday, December 17, 2021, 08:13 PM EST |
| Duration: | 44m 43s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 50 |
| Reactions: | 3 likes &#128077; and 1 dislike &#128078; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Joyce.  
>  
>  
>  
>Third prayer -- Dec. 17 (modern)  
>  
>O Admirable Leader, you have guided the people to obey   
>you, not by harshness but by the tenderness of your love.   
>We implore you, through your pure nativity and through   
>the  intercession  of  your  Mother  and  Saint  Joseph,  your   
>chosen  one,  to  teach  us  perfect  obedience  to  your  holy   
>commandments  and  to  our  superiors,  not  for  fear  of   
>punishment,  or  constraint,  or  external  force,  but  by  the   
>assent  of  our  minds,  the  surrender  of  our  hearts,  the   
>enthusiasm of our spirits, and the joy of our souls. Amen.  
>  
>  
>  
>  
>  
>  
>Third oration -- Dec. 17 (classic)  
>  
>O Admirable Leader Who gains the obedience of Your people not by the severity of Your judgment  
>but by the sweetness of Your love and Your welcome sojourn among us.  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to teach us complete obedience to Your holy commandments  
>and to submit to our superiors not for fear of punishment  
>but by a willing surrender of mind and heart, with gladness of heart and spirit.  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2021-12-16-19-32-42}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/ZankmID4c3I/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=ZankmID4c3I](https://youtube.com/watch?v=ZankmID4c3I) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Thursday, December 16, 2021, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Thursday, December 16, 2021, 07:32 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Thursday, December 16, 2021, 10:06 PM EST |
| Duration: | 56m 40s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 36 |
| Reactions: | 1 like &#128077; and 1 dislike &#128078; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Marielle.  
>  
>Second oration -- Dec 16:  
>  
>O Hope of the Patriarchs and longing of the Gentiles,  
>in Your Nativity You have granted us hope.  
>The joy of this hope has called together the Shepherds,   
>the Magi and all believers in Your Holy Name,  
>and led them to adore You with all the acclaim of their hearts.   
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Virgin Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to keep us, by Your grace, from attachment to earthly goods.  
>Teach us not to depend only on ourselves and the weak ones of this world,  
>but to rely only on Your Fatherly direction and Your Divine Providence.   
>Attract our hearts and minds to reflect on Your heavenly riches   
>and to aspire and long for them above.  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2021-12-15-19-28-48}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/mhXwGdcYhfE/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=mhXwGdcYhfE](https://youtube.com/watch?v=mhXwGdcYhfE) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Wednesday, December 15, 2021, 07:30 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Wednesday, December 15, 2021, 07:28 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Wednesday, December 15, 2021, 10:38 PM EST |
| Duration: | 51m 30s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 36 |
| Reactions: | 2 likes &#128077; and 1 dislike &#128078; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Joseph.  
>  
>First oration -- Dec. 15:  
>  
>O Beautiful One in splendor,  
>fairer than the children of this world,   
>in Your inexplicable love for us, You became a man  
>to show us the invisible beauty of Your Divinity  
>in the splendor of Your visible body,  
>which You have taken from the Virgin Mary.  
>By this, You have attracted us to Your Divine Love,  
>You, Beloved of the Father and Only Delight of His Heart.   
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Virgin Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to enkindle Your love in our hearts  
>and enlighten our minds with the rays of Your heavenly grace,  
>that we may love You with our whole heart and mind.  
>May we love everyone in You and for You,   
>You, Who alone are worthy of the love of all hearts,  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2020-12-23-18-59-54}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/Ueag2b5j4Qk/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=Ueag2b5j4Qk](https://youtube.com/watch?v=Ueag2b5j4Qk) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Wednesday, December 23, 2020, 07:00 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Wednesday, December 23, 2020, 06:59 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Wednesday, December 23, 2020, 09:51 PM EST |
| Duration: | 53m 55s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 47 |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by William  
>  
>Ninth prayer -- Dec. 23: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  though  you  are  rich  by  nature,  you  loved  the   
>poverty  of  our  humanity  when  you  became  man  like  us.   
>Though you were the descendent of kings and the heir of   
>David, the venerable, you were satisfied with a grotto and   
>a  humble  manger.    We  implore  you,  through  your  pure   
>nativity and through the intercession of your mother and   
>Saint  Joseph,  your  chosen  one,  to  give  us  a  love  of   
>voluntary  poverty,  that  we  may  be  satisfied  only  with   
>what  is  necessary  for  our  existence,  avoiding  waste  and   
>the love of an easy life, all the days of our lives. Amen.    
>  
>  
>  
>Ninth Oration -- Dec. 23: (classic)  
>  
>0 God, Who in Your very nature contain all the riches of heaven and earth,  
>You loved the poverty of humanity by choosing to become one of us,  
>You are the descendant of Kings and the Heir of David the Venerable.  
>You were satisfied to be born a stable and a humble manger.  
>   
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity   
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us an appreciation of voluntary poverty.  
>May we be satisfied with only that which is necessary for the maintenance of our lives.  
>Teach us to flee from excessive luxury and the love of abundance all the days of our lives.  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2020-12-22-18-57-37}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/mC_mPnBgszw/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=mC_mPnBgszw](https://youtube.com/watch?v=mC_mPnBgszw) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Tuesday, December 22, 2020, 07:00 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Tuesday, December 22, 2020, 06:57 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Tuesday, December 22, 2020, 09:29 PM EST |
| Duration: | 54m 25s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 44 |
| Reactions: | 2 likes &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Julian.  
>  
>Eighth prayer -- Dec. 22: (modern)  
>  
>O King of Great Counsel, you have united the magnificence   
>of  your  resplendent  power  with  the  prudence  of  human   
>judgment when you, the almighty and powerful God, took   
>flight to Egypt to escape Herod. We implore you, through   
>your  pure  nativity  and  through  the  intercession  of  your   
>mother  and  Saint  Joseph,  your  chosen  one,  to  grant  us   
>sound judgment in all of our actions, that we may act in all   
>wisdom and serve you all the days of our lives. Amen.    
>  
>  
>  
>Eighth oration -- Dec. 22: (classic)  
>  
>O King of Great Counsel,  
>You joined Your admirable power with the prudence of humuan judgment  
>when You, the Mighty and AII-Powerful God, fled into Egypt from the face of Herod.  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,   
>to grant us good judgment in all our actions,  
>that we may think and act wisely all the days of our lives  
>as we subject ourselves to Your divine service.  
>  
>Amen

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2020-12-21-19-00-04}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/Tk2HUDnZZJg/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=Tk2HUDnZZJg](https://youtube.com/watch?v=Tk2HUDnZZJg) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Monday, December 21, 2020, 07:00 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Monday, December 21, 2020, 07:00 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Monday, December 21, 2020, 09:39 PM EST |
| Duration: | 54m 5s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 44 |
| Reactions: | 1 like &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Nisreen.  
>  
>Seventh prayer -- Dec. 21: (modern)  
>  
>O  Lord,  you  are  one  person  who  has  two  natures,  and   
>have taught us what you have heard from the Father. We   
>implore you, through your pure nativity and through the   
>intercession of your Mother and Saint Joseph, your chosen   
>one,  to  grant  us  a  living  faith  and  good  works  in   
>accordance  with  that  faith.  Do  not  permit  us  to  lose  the   
>reward of our faith because of our wrong doings, but may   
>we bear the fruits of true faith and good works. Amen.  
>  
>  
>Seventh oration -- Dec. 21: (classic)  
>  
>0 You Who are One person but also have the Nature of man  
>You Who have told us what You have heard from the Father;   
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity and the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One  
>to grant us an ardent belief in Your teachings and good acts to harmonize with them.  
>Do not permit us to lose the reward of our faith because of our own wrong doings.  
>Rather, make our lives fruitful in beliefs and good works.  
>  
>Amen

#### The Genealogy of Jesus[]{#d2020-12-20-11-16-22}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/9DZhrmi8v1s/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=9DZhrmi8v1s](https://youtube.com/watch?v=9DZhrmi8v1s) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Sunday, December 20, 2020, 11:30 AM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Sunday, December 20, 2020, 11:16 AM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Sunday, December 20, 2020, 02:44 PM EST |
| Duration: | 1h 40m 35s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 171 |
| Reactions: | 5 likes &#128077; |

>December 20, 2020: The Genealogy of Jesus.  
>Today's Divine Liturgy can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/sunday.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/sunday.pdf) and the novena at http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf.  
>  
>Page 66 -- The Genealogy of Jesus  
>Page 748 -- Creed  
>Page 754 -- Anaphora of the Twelve Apostles  
>  
>We will pray Christmas Novena, (sixth day, Dec. 20) after the Divine Litugry.   The text can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf)  
>  
>This week's bulletin can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/201220-bulletin.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/201220-bulletin.pdf)  
>  
>  
>  
>Sixth Prayer of the Christmas Novena: (modern text)  
>  
>O Word of God, you have come from the mouth of God to   
>be the life for all people. You became the living bread and   
>were born in Bethlehem, “the House of Bread”, to satisfy   
>our  hunger.  We  implore  you,  through  your  pure  nativity   
>and  through  the  intercession  of  your  Mother  and  Saint   
>Joseph, your chosen one, to grant us an insatiable hunger   
>for  your  pure  Body  and  Blood.    May  we  approach  your   
>altars  and  with  proper  preparation receive your Sacred   
>Mysteries for our salvation and eternal life. Amen.  
>  
>  
>  
>  
>Sixth Oration of the Christmas Novena: (classic text)  
>  
>O Word of God, Who comes from the Mouth of God to be the  Life of all men;  
>You Who became Living Bread and was born in Bethlehem, "The House of Bread",  
>to satisfy our hunger.  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,  
>to grant us a piercing hunger for that Bread which is Your Pure Body and Blood.  
>May we ever approach Your altar and receive Your Sacred Mysteries  
>with fitting preparation so that our Communion may be for us salvation and life everlasting.  
>  
>Amen.

#### The Christmas Novena[]{#d2020-12-19-19-01-37}

![](https://i.ytimg.com/vi/iKXThl2RrgE/mqdefault.jpg)

|||
|-----|------|
| Video link: | [https://youtube.com/watch?v=iKXThl2RrgE](https://youtube.com/watch?v=iKXThl2RrgE) |
| Scheduled Start Time: | Saturday, December 19, 2020, 07:00 PM EST |
| Actual Start Time: | Saturday, December 19, 2020, 07:01 PM EST |
| Actual End Time: | Saturday, December 19, 2020, 09:30 PM EST |
| Duration: | 53m 5s |
| Visibility:    | unlisted          |
| Views: | 74 |
| Reactions: | 5 likes &#128077; |

>The Christmas Novena prayers can be found at [http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf](http://ololc.org/doc/novena.pdf).  
>Cover art by Matthew.  
>  
>Fifth prayer -- Dec. 19: (modern)  
>  
>O Mighty One, seated in the heavenly heights, you left the   
>glory  of  your  divinity  and  loved  the  lowliness  of  our   
>human  nature  to  become  our  model  of  humility  and   
>lowliness.  We  implore  you,  through  your  pure  nativity   
>and  through  the  intercession  of  your  Mother  and  Saint   
>Joseph, your chosen one, to give us humility of heart and   
>the  denial  of  ourselves,  that  we  may  remove  all  pride   
>which  prevents  us  from  knowing  you.  We  would  then   
>realize the weakness of our condition and glorify you, for   
>you  alone  are  holy  and  great  is  your  name.  To  you  be   
>glory, power, and strength. Amen.    
>  
>  
>Fifth oration -- Dec. 19: (classic)  
>  
>O Most High, by nature supreme and outranking all men and all things,  
>Who has left the magnificence of Your Divinity  
>and loved the lowliness of our humanity  
>to become for us a model in humility and lowliness;  
>  
>We beseech You, through Your Pure Nativity  
>and through the intercession of Your Mother and Saint Joseph, Your Chosen One,   
>to grant us humility of heart and an accurate estimation of ourselves.  
>Help us to conquer every show of false pride,  
>which would have us choose our own whims rather than Your Will.   
>May we realize that, compared to You, we are little indeed.  
>  
>Glory be to You, for You alone are holy and great is Your Name.  
>To You be Glory, Magnificence and Power!  
>  
>Amen
